        So Black has two pawns under attack.
        Pawn a5 is attacked by Rook a1.
        Pawn g6 is attacked by Rook d6.
        Black can't save both in one move.
        Wait, can `g6g5` also attack something?
        Yes, the pawn at h4.
        So `g6g5` saves a pawn (g6) and attacks another (h4).
        `a5a4` moves a pawn (a5) and doesn't attack anything.

        Between the choices, `g6g5` seems like the strongest move because it's active.
----- END REASONING (Player_Black) -----
